Lilioceris atrilateralis
    [P54.s0] Lilioceris atrilateralis Kimoto & Takizawa, 1973: 171 (Nepal).
    [P56.s0] **Remarks.** This species is very similar to L. semipunctata according to the original description and photographs of the holotype, but differs by the black femora with its middle brownish red in ventral view; sides of the abdomen are black.
        type: femora  (shared by 2 facts)
            value: black with its middle brownish red in ventral view  [loose]
                who: L. semipunctata
        type: sides of the abdomen
            value: black
                who: Lilioceris atrilateralis  [not printed]
        (f2 is of L. semipunctata)
    [P56.s1] In L. semipunctata, the femora are brownish red; sides of the abdomen are brownish red.
        (f3 is of L. semipunctata)
